0\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 1}}\right)}}=\\
=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{1+{x_{5}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{1+{x_{5}}}&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{x_{5}}}}{{1+{x_{5}}}}\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{l}{t_{5}}={\left[{B,E;D,F}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1+{x_{4}}+{z_{4}}\\
1\\
{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\\
=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=-\frac{{{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}}{{1-{y_{4}}-{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}}\end{array}

After multiplying by the denominators and omitting the negligible terms we get:

{x_{1}}\approx-{z_{4}}

{y_{1}}{t_{4}}\approx{y_{4}}

{z_{1}}\approx 0

0\approx-{x_{4}}

{x_{1}}\approx-{z_{5}}

{y_{1}}{y_{5}}\approx 0

{t_{1}}\approx{x_{5}}

{t_{5}}\approx-{y_{4}}

We may conclude

d{x_{1}}=-d{z_{4}}

{y_{1}}\cdot d{t_{4}}=d{y_{4}}

d{z_{1}}=0

d{x_{4}}=0

d{x_{1}}=-d{z_{5}}

{y_{1}}\cdot d{y_{5}}=0

d{t_{1}}=d{x_{5}}

dt_{5}=-dy_{4}

The last four equations allow us to eliminate
dx_{5},dy_{5},dz_{5},dt_{5}
.
The former four equation allow to eliminate

dz_{1},dx_{4},dy_{4},dz_{4}
. SO the cotangent space is spanned by
dx_{1},dy_{1},dt_{1},dt_{4}
.

5.6
\mathcal{C}.2

We shall not discuss the last two charts, as it is obvious all that the first
three charts contain all the cross ratios and define the last charts completely.
On the first three charts, we choose the following coordinates:

\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{1}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{1}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{C_{1}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{D_{1}}=\left({{x_{1}}:1:{y_{1}}}\right)\\
{E_{1}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{F_{1}}=\left({{z_{1}}{t_{1}}:{z_{1}}:1}\right)\\
{x_{1}},{z_{1}},{t_{1}}\sim 0\neq{y_{1}}\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{2}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{2}}=\left({1:{x_{2}}:{x_{2}}{y_{2}}}\right)\\
{C_{2}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{D_{2}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{E_{2}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{F_{2}}=\left({{z_{2}}:1+{t_{2}}:1}\right)\\
{x_{2}},{z_{2}},{t_{2}}\sim 0\neq{y_{2}}\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{3}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{3}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{C_{3}}=\left({1:1+{x_{3}}:{y_{3}}}\right)\\
{D_{3}}=\left(1:1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}\right.\\
\hfill\left.:{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}\right)\\
{E_{3}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{F_{3}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{x_{3}},{y_{3}},{z_{3}},{t_{3}}\sim 0\end{array}\end{array}

We write the formulas relating the coordinates of different charts.

\begin{array}[]{l}{y_{1}}={\left[{C,E;D,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
{x_{2}}\\
{x_{2}}{y_{2}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{x_{2}}}\\
1&{{x_{2}}{y_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{x_{2}}}\\
0&{{x_{2}}{y_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{x_{2}}\left({{y_{2}}-1}\right)}}{{{x_{2}}{y_{2}}}}=\frac{{{y_{2}}-1}}{{{y_{2}}}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{z_{1}}={\left[{E,C;F,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{2}}\\
1+{t_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
{x_{2}}\\
{x_{2}}{y_{2}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{1+{t_{2}}}\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{x_{2}}}\\
0&{{x_{2}}{y_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{x_{2}}}\\
1&{{x_{2}}{y_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{1+{t_{2}}}\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{t_{2}}\cdot{x_{2}}{y_{2}}}}{{{x_{2}}\left({1-{y_{2}}}\right)}}=\frac{{{y_{2}}{t_{2}}}}{{1-{y_{2}}}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{t_{1}}={\left[{C,A;F,B}\right]_{E}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{2}}\\
1+{t_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
{x_{2}}\\
{x_{2}}{y_{2}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 1\atop\scriptstyle 1}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{z_{2}}}&1\\
1&{1+{t_{2}}}&1\\
0&1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1&1\\
0&{{x_{2}}}&1\\
0&{{x_{2}}{y_{2}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&1\\
1&{{x_{2}}}&1\\
0&{{x_{2}}{y_{2}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{z_{2}}}&1\\
0&{1+{t_{2}}}&1\\
0&1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{\left({1-{z_{2}}}\right)\cdot{x_{2}}\left({1-{y_{2}}}\right)}}{{\left({{x_{2}}{y_{2}}-1}\right){t_{2}}}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{x_{1}}={\left[{C,A;D,B}\right]_{E}}={\left[\footnotesize{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1+{x_{3}}\\
{y_{3}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}\\
{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 1}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{1+{x_{3}}}&{1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
{1+{x_{3}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&{1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}}{{1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{y_{1}}={\left[{C,E;D,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[\footnotesize{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1+{x_{3}}\\
{y_{3}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}\\
{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\\
=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{3}}}&{1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\\
{{y_{3}}}&{{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{3}}}&1\\
{{y_{3}}}&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\\
1&{{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{-\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{3}}}&{{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\\
{{y_{3}}}&{{z_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{{y_{3}}\left({1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{-{z_{3}}\left({1+{x_{3}}-{y_{3}}{t_{3}}}\right)}}{{{y_{3}}\left({1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\right)}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{z_{1}}={\left[{E,C;F,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1+{x_{3}}\\
{y_{3}}\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\\
=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{3}}}&1\\
{{y_{3}}}&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{3}}}&1\\
{{y_{3}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=-\frac{{{y_{3}}}}{{1+{x_{3}}-{y_{3}}}}\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{l}{t_{1}}={\left[{C,A;F,B}\right]_{E}}={\left[\small{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1+{x_{3}}\\
{y_{3}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 1}}\right)}}=\\
=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{1+{x_{3}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
{1+{x_{3}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=-{x_{3}}\end{array}

{z_{2}}={\left[{D,A;F,E}\right]_{C}}={\left[\tiny{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}\\
{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 1+{x_{3}}\atop\scriptstyle{y_{3}}}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1&1\\
{1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}&1&{1+{x_{3}}}\\
{{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}}&1&{{y_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0&1\\
0&0&{1+{x_{3}}}\\
0&1&{{y_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0&1\\
{1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}&0&{1+{x_{3}}}\\
{{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}}&1&{{y_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1&1\\
0&1&{1+{x_{3}}}\\
0&1&{{y_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&1\\
{{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}&1&{1+{x_{3}}}\\
{{z_{3}}}&1&{{y_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left({1+{x_{3}}}\right)}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&0&1\\
{{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}&0&{1+{x_{3}}}\\
{{z_{3}}}&1&{{y_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left({1+{x_{3}}-{y_{3}}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&1\\
{{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}&0&{{x_{3}}}\\
{{z_{3}}}&0&{{y_{3}}-1}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left({1+{x_{3}}}\right)}}{{{z_{3}}{t_{3}}\cdot\left({1+{x_{3}}-{y_{3}}}\right)}}=\frac{{\left({{x_{3}}-{t_{3}}{y_{3}}+{t_{3}}}\right)\cdot\left({1+{x_{3}}}\right)}}{{{t_{3}}\cdot\left({1+{x_{3}}-{y_{3}}}\right)}}

After multiplying by the denominators and omitting the negligible terms, we get

{y_{1}}{y_{2}}={y_{2}}-1

{z_{1}}\left({1-{y_{2}}}\right)={y_{2}}{t_{2}}

0\approx{x_{2}}\left({1-{y_{2}}}\right)

{x_{1}}\approx 0

{y_{1}}{y_{3}}\approx-{z_{3}}

{z_{1}}\approx-{y_{3}}

{t_{1}}=-{x_{3}}

0\approx{x_{3}}+{t_{3}}

We conclude

y_{2}dy_{1}=(1-y_{1})dy_{2}

\left({1-{y_{2}}}\right)dz_{1}={y_{2}}dt_{2}

0=\left({1-{y_{2}}}\right)dx_{2}

dx_{1}=0

y_{3}dy_{1}=-dz_{3}

dz_{1}=-dy_{3}

dt_{1}=-dx_{3}

0=d{x_{3}}+d{t_{3}}

The last equation may be used to eliminate
dt_{3}
, the 3 equations before it may be used to exclude
dx_{3},dy_{3},dz_{3}
.
The first four among the last eight equations allow to exclude
dx_{1},dx_{2},dy_{2},dt_{2}
.
So the cotangent bundle is spanned
dy_{1},dz_{1},dt_{1},dz_{2}
.

5.7
\mathcal{C}.4

The information in the charts 2 and 4 follows from charts 1, 3, and 5.

\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{1}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{1}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{C_{1}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{D_{1}}=\left({{x_{1}}:1:{y_{1}}}\right)\\
{E_{1}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{F_{1}}=\left({{z_{1}}{t_{1}}:{z_{1}}:1}\right)\\
{x_{1}},{z_{1}},{t_{1}}\sim 0\neq{y_{1}}\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{3}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{3}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{C_{3}}=\left({1+{x_{3}}:1:{y_{3}}}\right)\\
{D_{3}}=\left(1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}\right.\\
\hfill\left.:1:{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}\right)\\
{E_{3}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{F_{3}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{x_{3}},{y_{3}},{z_{3}}\sim 0\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{5}}=\left({1:1+{x_{5}}:{y_{5}}}\right)\\
{B_{5}}=\left({1:1+{x_{5}}+{z_{5}}}\right.\\
\left.{\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,:{y_{5}}+{z_{5}}{t_{5}}}\right)\\
{C_{5}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{D_{5}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{E_{5}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{F_{5}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{x_{5}},{y_{5}},{z_{5}},{t_{5}}\sim 0\end{array}\end{array}

The formulas connecting different charts:

\begin{array}[]{l}{x_{1}}={\left[{C,A;D,B}\right]_{E}}={\left[\footnotesize{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1+{x_{3}}\\
1\\
{y_{3}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}\\
1\\
{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 1}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{3}}}&{1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}}\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{3}}}&0\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}}\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{-{z_{3}}}}{{1+{x_{3}}}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{y_{1}}={\left[{C,E;D,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[\footnotesize{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1+{x_{3}}\\
1\\
{y_{3}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}\\
1\\
{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{{y_{3}}}&{{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{{y_{3}}}&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&{{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{-{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}}{{{y_{3}}}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{z_{1}}={\left[{E,C;F,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1+{x_{3}}\\
1\\
{y_{3}}\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{{y_{3}}}&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{{y_{3}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{y_{3}}}}{{{y_{3}}-1}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{t_{1}}={\left[{C,A;F,B}\right]_{E}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1+{x_{3}}\\
1\\
{y_{3}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 1}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{3}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{3}}}&0\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{x_{3}}}}{{1+{x_{3}}}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{x_{1}}={\left[{C,A;D,B}\right]_{E}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1+{x_{5}}\\
{y_{5}}\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1+{x_{5}}+{z_{5}}\\
{y_{5}}+{z_{5}}{t_{5}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 1}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{1+{x_{5}}}&{1+{x_{5}}+{z_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&{1+{x_{5}}+{z_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
{1+{x_{5}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{z_{5}}}}{{1+{x_{5}}+{z_{5}}}}

{y_{1}}={\left[{C,E;D,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1+{x_{5}}+{z_{5}}\\
{y_{5}}+{z_{5}}{t_{5}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 1+{x_{5}}\atop\scriptstyle{y_{5}}}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0&1\\
0&1&{1+{x_{5}}}\\
0&0&{{y_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&1\\
0&{1+{x_{5}}+{z_{5}}}&{1+{x_{5}}}\\
1&{{y_{5}}+{z_{5}}{t_{5}}}&{{y_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1&1\\
0&{1+{x_{5}}+{z_{5}}}&{1+{x_{5}}}\\
0&{{y_{5}}+{z_{5}}{t_{5}}}&{{y_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&0&1\\
0&1&{1+{x_{5}}}\\
1&0&{{y_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=

=\frac{{{y_{5}}\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&0&1\\
0&{{z_{5}}}&{1+{x_{5}}}\\
1&{{z_{5}}{t_{5}}}&{{y_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{-\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0&1\\
0&{{z_{5}}}&{1+{x_{5}}}\\
0&{{z_{5}}{t_{5}}}&{{y_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{y_{5}}{z_{5}}}}{{{z_{5}}\left({{y_{5}}-{t_{5}}-{x_{5}}{t_{5}}}\right)}}=\frac{{{y_{5}}}}{{{y_{5}}-{t_{5}}-{x_{5}}{t_{5}}}}

{z_{1}}={\left[{E,C;F,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1+{x_{5}}+{z_{5}}\\
{y_{5}}+{z_{5}}{t_{5}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 1+{x_{5}}\atop\scriptstyle{y_{5}}}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&1\\
0&1&{1+{x_{5}}}\\
1&1&{{y_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1&1\\
0&{1+{x_{5}}+{z_{5}}}&{1+{x_{5}}}\\
0&{{y_{5}}+{z_{5}}{t_{5}}}&{{y_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&1\\
0&{1+{x_{5}}+{z_{5}}}&{1+{x_{5}}}\\
1&{{y_{5}}+{z_{5}}{t_{5}}}&{{y_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1&1\\
0&1&{1+{x_{5}}}\\
0&1&{{y_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=

=\frac{{{x_{5}}\cdot{z_{5}}\left({{y_{5}}-{t_{5}}-{x_{5}}{t_{5}}}\right)}}{{{z_{5}}\left({1+{x_{5}}-{y_{5}}}\right)}}=\frac{{{x_{5}}\left({{y_{5}}-{t_{5}}-{x_{5}}{t_{5}}}\right)}}{{1+{x_{5}}-{y_{5}}}}

{t_{1}}={\left[{C,A;F,B}\right]_{E}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1+{x_{5}}\\
{y_{5}}\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1+{x_{5}}+{z_{5}}\\
{y_{5}}+{z_{5}}{t_{5}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 1}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{1+{x_{5}}}&{1+{x_{5}}+{z_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&{1+{x_{5}}+{z_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{1+{x_{5}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{z_{5}}}}{{-{x_{5}}\left({1+{x_{5}}+{z_{5}}}\right)}}

As these equations produce linearly dependent conditions, we add another equation:

\begin{array}[]{l}{y_{5}}={\left[{C,E;A,F}\right]_{D}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1+{x_{3}}\\
1\\
{y_{3}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}\atop{\scriptstyle 1\atop\scriptstyle{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}}\right)}}=\\
=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{3}}}&1&{1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}}\\
1&0&1\\
{{y_{3}}}&0&{{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&{1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}}\\
0&1&1\\
1&1&{{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{3}}}&1&{1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}}\\
1&1&1\\
{{y_{3}}}&1&{{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&{1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}}\\
0&0&1\\
1&0&{{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{{z_{3}}{t_{3}}\left({{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}}\right)}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{3}}}&1&{{z_{3}}}\\
1&1&0\\
{{y_{3}}}&1&{{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{t_{3}}\left({{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}}\right)}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{3}}}&0&1\\
1&1&0\\
{{y_{3}}}&1&{{t_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{t_{3}}\left({{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}}\right)}}{{1-{y_{3}}+{x_{3}}{t_{3}}}}

After multiplying by the denominators, and omitting the negligible terms we get

{x_{1}}\approx-{z_{3}}

{y_{1}}{y_{3}}=-{z_{3}}{t_{3}}

{z_{1}}\approx-{y_{3}}

{t_{1}}\approx{x_{3}}

{x_{1}}\approx{z_{5}}

{y_{1}}\left({{y_{5}}-{t_{5}}}\right)\approx{y_{5}}

{z_{1}}\approx 0

0\approx{z_{5}}

{y_{5}}\approx{t_{3}}\left({{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}}\right)

It follows that

dx_{1}=-dz_{3}

y_{1}dy_{3}=-t_{3}dz_{3}

dz_{1}=-dy_{3}

dt_{1}=dx_{3}

dx_{1}=dz_{5}

0=(1-y_{1})dy_{5}+y_{1}dt_{5}

dz_{1}=0

0=dz_{5}

y_{5}\approx{t_{3}}\left({dx_{3}+dz_{3}}\right)

From the sixth equation we may express
dt_{5}
by
dy_{5}
and from the ninth equation we may express
dy_{5}
from the coordinates
related to the third chart, so we may omit the both. From the eighth equation we get that
dz_{5}
is zero,
hence by the fifth equation
dx_{1}
is zero and hence by the first equation
dz_{3}
is zero and by the second equation
dy_{3}
is zero,
and by the third equation (or directly by the seventh equation) also
dz_{1}
is zero.
So the cotangent space is spanned by
dy_{1},dt_{1},dx_{3},dt_{3},dx_{5}
. But the fourth equation relates
dt_{1}
to
dx_{3}
,
so the cotangent space is at most four-dimensional.

5.8
\mathcal{D}

We may choose local coordinates related to different charts:

\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{1}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{1}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{C_{1}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{D_{1}}=\left({{x_{1}}{y_{1}}:1:{x_{1}}}\right)\\
{E_{1}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{F_{1}}=\left({{z_{1}}{t_{1}}:1:{z_{1}}}\right)\\
{x_{1}},{y_{1}},{z_{1}},{t_{1}}\sim 0\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{2}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{2}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{C_{2}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{D_{2}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{E_{2}}=\left({{x_{2}}:1+{y_{2}}:1}\right)\\
{F_{2}}=\left({{z_{2}}:{t_{2}}:1}\right)\\
{x_{2}},{y_{2}},{z_{2}}\sim 0\neq{t_{2}}\end{array}\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{3}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{3}}=\left({1:{x_{3}}{y_{3}}:{x_{3}}}\right)\\
{C_{3}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{D_{3}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{E_{3}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{F_{3}}=\left({1:{z_{3}}{t_{3}}:{z_{3}}}\right)\\
{x_{3}},{y_{3}},{z_{3}}\sim 0\neq{t_{3}}\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{4}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{4}}=\left({1:{x_{4}}{y_{4}}:{x_{4}}}\right)\\
{C_{4}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{D_{4}}=\left({{t_{4}}:1:{z_{4}}}\right)\\
{E_{4}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{F_{4}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{x_{4}},{y_{4}},{t_{4}}\sim 0\neq{z_{4}}\end{array}\end{array}

We shall start by explaining that the coordinates of the first chart are related to the second chart,
then that the coordinates of the fourth charts are related to the other charts, in the end we shall relate
between the coordinates of the second and the third charts.

\begin{array}[]{l}{x_{1}}={\left[{C,E;D,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\
1+{y_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|.\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{y_{2}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|.\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{y_{2}}}&0\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{y_{2}}}}{{1+{y_{2}}}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{y_{1}}={\left[{E,A;D,B}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\
1+{y_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 1\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&0\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|.\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|.\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{x_{2}}}}{{{x_{2}}-1}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{z_{1}}={\left[{C,E;F,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\
1+{y_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{2}}\\
{t_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{t_{2}}}\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|.\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{y_{2}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|.\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{y_{2}}}&{{t_{2}}}\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{y_{2}}}}{{1+{y_{2}}-{t_{2}}}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{t_{1}}={\left[{E,A;F,B}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\
1+{y_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{2}}\\
{t_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 1\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&{{z_{2}}}\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|.\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|.\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{z_{2}}}\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{z_{2}}-{x_{2}}}}{{1-{x_{2}}}}

After multiplying by the denominators and omitting negligible terms, we get the equations

\begin{array}[]{l}{x_{1}}\approx{y_{2}}\\
{y_{1}}\approx-{x_{2}}\\
\left({1-{t_{2}}}\right){z_{1}}\approx{y_{2}}\\
{t_{1}}\approx{z_{2}}-{x_{2}}\end{array}

Since
1\neq t_{2}
, the coordinates of the first chart are locally expressed in terms of the second chart.

\begin{array}[]{l}{y_{3}}={\left[{E,C;B,D}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
{x_{4}}{y_{4}}\\
{x_{4}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{t_{4}}\\
1\\
{z_{4}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{x_{4}}{y_{4}}}\\
1&{{x_{4}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&{{z_{4}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&{{z_{4}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{x_{4}}{y_{4}}}\\
0&{{x_{4}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{x_{4}}{y_{4}}\cdot{z_{4}}}}{{{x_{4}}}}={y_{4}}{z_{4}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{z_{3}}={\left[{A,E;F,D}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{t_{4}}\\
1\\
{z_{4}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 1\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{t_{4}}}\\
1&{{z_{4}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{t_{4}}}\\
0&{{z_{4}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{t_{4}}}}{{{z_{4}}}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{t_{3}}={\left[{E,C;F,D}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{t_{4}}\\
1\\
{z_{4}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&{{z_{4}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&{{z_{4}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}={z_{4}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{x_{4}}={\left[{A,E;B,F}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\
1+{y_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{2}}\\
{t_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 1\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&{{z_{2}}}\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{z_{2}}}\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{z_{2}}-{x_{2}}}}{{1-{x_{2}}}}

After multiplying by the denominators and omitting negligible terms, we get

\begin{array}[]{l}{y_{3}}\approx{y_{4}}{z_{4}}\\
{z_{3}}{z_{4}}={t_{4}}\\
{t_{3}}={z_{4}}\\
{x_{4}}\approx{z_{2}}-{x_{2}}\end{array}

Passing to differentials we get:

\begin{array}[]{l}d{y_{3}}={z_{4}}\cdot d{y_{4}}\\
{z_{4}}\cdot d{z_{3}}=d{t_{4}}\\
d{t_{3}}=d{z_{4}}\\
d{x_{4}}=d{z_{2}}-d{x_{2}}\end{array}

We see that
dx_{4},dy_{4},dz_{4}
and
dt_{4}
are easily expressed, since
z_{4}\neq 0
.
So we are down to 8 coordinates related to the second and third maps.

\begin{array}[]{l}{x_{3}}={\left[{A,E;B,D}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\
1+{y_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 1\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&0\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{x_{2}}}}{{{x_{2}}-1}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{y_{3}}={\left[{E,C;B,D}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\
1+{y_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{y_{2}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{y_{2}}}&0\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{y_{2}}}}{{1+{y_{2}}}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{z_{3}}={\left[{A,E;F,D}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\
1+{y_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{2}}\\
{t_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 1\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{z_{2}}}\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&0\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&{{z_{2}}}\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{x_{2}}}}{{{x_{2}}-{z_{2}}}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{t_{3}}={\left[{E,C;F,D}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\
1+{y_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{2}}\\
{t_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{y_{2}}}&{{t_{2}}}\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{y_{2}}}&0\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{t_{2}}}\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{1+{y_{2}}-{t_{2}}}}{{1+{y_{2}}}}

After getting rid of the denominators and omitting the negligible terms we get:

\begin{array}[]{l}{x_{3}}\approx-{x_{2}}\\
{y_{3}}\approx{y_{2}}\\
0\approx{x_{2}}\\
{t_{3}}\approx 1-{t_{2}}\left({1-{y_{2}}}\right)\end{array}

In terms of differentials:

\begin{array}[]{l}d{x_{3}}=-d{x_{2}}\\
d{y_{3}}=d{y_{2}}\\
0=d{x_{2}}\\
d{t_{3}}=-d{t_{2}}+{t_{2}}\cdot d{y_{2}}\end{array}

So
dx_{2}
and
dx_{3}
are zeroes,
dy_{3}
and
dt_{3}
are expressed from the equations of the second chart,
so we are down to
dy_{2},dz_{2},dt_{2}
and
dz_{3}
and hence the cotangent space is at most 4-dimensional.

5.9
\mathcal{E}.1

The local coordinates for related to the charts are:

\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{1}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{1}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{C_{1}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{D_{1}}=\left({{x_{1}}{y_{1}}:1:{x_{1}}}\right)\\
{E_{1}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{F_{1}}=\left({{z_{1}}{t_{1}}:1:{z_{1}}}\right)\\
{x_{1}},{y_{1}},{z_{1}}\sim 0\neq{t_{1}}\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{2}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{2}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{C_{2}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{D_{2}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{E_{2}}=\left({{x_{2}}:1+{y_{2}}:1}\right)\\
{F_{2}}=\left({{z_{2}}:1:{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}\right)\\
{x_{2}},{y_{2}},{z_{2}}\sim 0\neq{t_{2}}\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{3}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{3}}=\left({1:{x_{3}}{y_{3}}:{x_{3}}}\right)\\
{C_{3}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{D_{3}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{E_{3}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{F_{3}}=\left({1:{z_{3}}:{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\right)\\
{x_{3}},{y_{3}},{z_{3}},{t_{3}}\sim 0\end{array}\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{4}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{4}}=\left({1:{x_{4}}:{y_{4}}}\right)\\
{C_{4}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{D_{4}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{E_{4}}=\left({{z_{4}}{t_{4}}:{z_{4}}:1}\right)\\
{F_{4}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{x_{4}},{z_{4}},{t_{4}}\sim 0\neq{y_{4}}\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{5}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{5}}=\left({1:{x_{5}}:{x_{5}}{y_{5}}}\right)\\
{C_{5}}=\left({{z_{5}}:1:1+{t_{5}}}\right)\\
{D_{5}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{E_{5}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{F_{5}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{x_{5}},{y_{5}},{z_{5}},{t_{5}}\sim 0\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{6}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{6}}=\left({1:{x_{6}}:{x_{6}}}\right)\\
{C_{6}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{D_{6}}=\left({{y_{6}}:1:{y_{6}}}\right)\\
{E_{6}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{F_{6}}=\left({1:{z_{6}}:{t_{6}}}\right)\\
{x_{6}},{y_{6}},{t_{6}}\sim 0\neq{z_{6}}\end{array}\end{array}

We start by relating the first chart to the second:

\begin{array}[]{l}{x_{1}}={\left[{C,E;D,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\
1+{y_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{y_{2}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{y_{2}}}&0\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{y_{2}}}}{{1+{y_{2}}}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{y_{1}}={\left[{E,A;D,B}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\
1+{y_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 1\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&0\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{x_{2}}}}{{-1+{x_{2}}}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{z_{1}}={\left[{C,E;F,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\
1+{y_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{2}}\\
1\\
{z_{2}}{t_{2}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&{{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{y_{2}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{y_{2}}}&1\\
1&{{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=-\frac{{{z_{2}}{t_{2}}{y_{2}}}}{{1-{z_{2}}{t_{2}}\left({1+{y_{2}}}\right)}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{t_{1}}={\left[{E,A;F,B}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\
1+{y_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{2}}\\
1\\
{z_{2}}{t_{2}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 1\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&{{z_{2}}}\\
1&{{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{z_{2}}}\\
0&{{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&1\\
1&{{t_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&{{t_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{1-{x_{2}}{t_{2}}}}{{\left({1-{x_{2}}}\right){t_{2}}}}

Notice that all the denominators in the last 4 formulas are non-zero,
when
{x_{2}},{y_{2}},{z_{2}}
are near zero, and
t_{2}
is non-zero.

Hence
dx_{1},dy_{1}.dz_{1},dt_{1}
can be expressed in terms of coordinates related to the second chart.
So from now on we may disregard the first chart.

Now we connect the coordinates related to the third chart to the coordinates related to the fifth chart.

\begin{array}[]{l}{x_{3}}={\left[{A,E;B,D}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
{x_{5}}\\
{x_{5}}{y_{5}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle{z_{5}}\atop{\scriptstyle 1\atop\scriptstyle 1+{t_{5}}}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1&{{z_{5}}}\\
0&{{x_{5}}}&1\\
0&{{x_{5}}{y_{5}}}&{1+{t_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&0&{{z_{5}}}\\
1&0&1\\
0&1&{1+{t_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0&{{z_{5}}}\\
0&0&1\\
0&1&{1+{t_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&{{z_{5}}}\\
1&{{x_{5}}}&1\\
0&{{x_{5}}{y_{5}}}&{1+{t_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{x_{5}}\left({1+{t_{5}}-{y_{5}}}\right)\cdot{z_{5}}}}{{1+{t_{5}}-{x_{5}}{y_{5}}{z_{5}}}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{y_{3}}={\left[{E,C;B,D}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{5}}\\
1\\
1+{t_{5}}\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
{x_{5}}\\
{x_{5}}{y_{5}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{x_{5}}}\\
0&{{x_{5}}{y_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
{1+{t_{5}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{x_{5}}}\\
{1+{t_{5}}}&{{x_{5}}{y_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&{{y_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{z_{5}}}&1\\
{1+{t_{5}}}&{{y_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=-\frac{{{y_{5}}}}{{1+{t_{5}}-{y_{5}}{z_{5}}}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{z_{3}}={\left[{A,C;F,D}\right]_{E}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{5}}\\
1\\
1+{t_{5}}\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 1\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{z_{5}}}&0\\
{1+{t_{5}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{z_{5}}}&1\\
{1+{t_{5}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=-\frac{{{z_{5}}}}{{1+{t_{5}}-{z_{5}}}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{t_{3}}={\left[{C,E;F,D}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{5}}\\
1\\
1+{t_{5}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{1+{t_{5}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
{1+{t_{5}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=-{t_{5}}

Notice that all the denominators in the last 4 formulas are non-zero,
when
x_{5},y_{5},z_{5},t_{5}
are near zero.
Hence
dx_{3},dy_{3},dz_{3},dt_{3}
can be expressed in terms of coordinates related to the fifth chart.
So from now on we may disregard the third chart.

So we have only 4 charts to consider: the second, the fourth, the fifth, and the sixth.
We start with relating the fifth chart to the others:

\begin{array}[]{l}{x_{4}}={\left[{A,C;B,F}\right]_{D}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{5}}\\
1\\
1+{t_{5}}\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
{x_{5}}\\
{x_{5}}{y_{5}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 1}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&{{x_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{z_{5}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{z_{5}}}&1\\
1&{{x_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{x_{5}}\left({1-{z_{5}}}\right)}}{{1-{x_{5}}{z_{5}}}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{y_{5}}={\left[{E,D;B,F}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\
1+{y_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{2}}\\
1\\
{z_{2}}{t_{2}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{y_{2}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&{{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{y_{2}}}&1\\
1&{{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=-\frac{{{y_{2}}}}{{1-{z_{2}}{t_{2}}\left({1+{y_{2}}}\right)}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{z_{4}}={\left[{D,C;E,F}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{5}}\\
1\\
1+{t_{5}}\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{1+{t_{5}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{1+{t_{5}}}&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{t_{5}}}}{{1+{t_{5}}}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{t_{4}}={\left[{C,A;E,F}\right]_{D}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{5}}\\
1\\
1+{t_{5}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 1}}\right)}}\,=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{z_{5}}}&0\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{z_{5}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{z_{5}}}}{{{z_{5}}-1}}

After getting rid of denominators we get:

{x_{4}}\approx{x_{5}}

{y_{5}}\approx{y_{2}}

{z_{4}}\approx{t_{5}}

{t_{4}}\approx-{z_{5}}

This allows us to express
dz_{5},dy_{5},dz_{5},dt_{5}
in terms of the coordinates from second and fourth chart.
Lastly, we connect the coordinates from the second, the fourth, and the sixth charts:

\begin{array}[]{l}{x_{2}}={\left[{D,A;E,B}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{4}}{t_{4}}\\
{z_{4}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
{x_{4}}\\
{y_{4}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 1\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&{{y_{4}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&{{y_{4}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}={y_{4}}{z_{4}}{t_{4}}

\begin{array}[]{l}1+{y_{2}}={\left[{D,C;E,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{4}}{t_{4}}\\
{z_{4}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
{x_{4}}\\
{y_{4}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{z_{4}}}\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{x_{4}}}\\
0&{{y_{4}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{x_{4}}}\\
1&{{y_{4}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{z_{4}}}\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{z_{4}}{y_{4}}}}{{{x_{4}}}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{z_{2}}={\left[{C,A;F,B}\right]_{D}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
{x_{4}}\\
{y_{4}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 1}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&{{x_{4}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&{{x_{4}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}={x_{4}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{t_{2}}={\left[{A,D;F,B}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
{x_{4}}\\
{y_{4}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 1\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&{{y_{4}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&{{y_{4}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{1}{{{y_{4}}}}

1+{y_{2}}={\left[{D,C;E,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{y_{6}}\\
1\\
{y_{6}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
{x_{6}}\\
{x_{6}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
{{y_{6}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{x_{6}}}\\
0&{{x_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{x_{6}}}\\
{{y_{6}}}&{{x_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{{y_{6}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{1}{{1-{y_{6}}}}

{z_{2}}={\left[{C,A;F,B}\right]_{D}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
{z_{6}}\\
{t_{6}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
{x_{6}}\\
{x_{6}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle{y_{6}}\atop{\scriptstyle 1\atop\scriptstyle{y_{6}}}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&{{y_{6}}}\\
1&{{z_{6}}}&1\\
0&{{t_{6}}}&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1&{{y_{6}}}\\
0&{{x_{6}}}&1\\
0&{{x_{6}}}&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&{{y_{6}}}\\
1&{{x_{6}}}&1\\
0&{{x_{6}}}&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1&{{y_{6}}}\\
0&{{z_{6}}}&1\\
0&{{t_{6}}}&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{y_{6}}}\\
{{t_{6}}}&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{x_{6}}&1\\
{x_{6}}&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{y_{6}}}\\
{{x_{6}}}&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{z_{6}}}&1\\
{{t_{6}}}&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{{t_{6}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot{x_{6}}\left({1-{y_{6}}}\right)}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{{x_{6}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left({{t_{6}}-{y_{6}}{z_{6}}}\right)}}=\frac{{\left({1-{t_{6}}}\right)\cdot{x_{6}}\left({1-{y_{6}}}\right)}}{{\left({1-{x_{6}}}\right)\cdot\left({{t_{6}}-{y_{6}}{z_{6}}}\right)}}

{t_{2}}={\left[{A,D;F,B}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{y_{6}}\\
1\\
{y_{6}}\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
{z_{6}}\\
{t_{6}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
{x_{6}}\\
{x_{6}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 1\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&{{t_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{y_{6}}}&1\\
{{y_{6}}}&{{x_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&{{x_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{y_{6}}}&1\\
{{y_{6}}}&{{t_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{t_{6}}\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
1&{{x_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{{x_{6}}\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
1&{{t_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{t_{6}}\left({1-{x_{6}}}\right)}}{{{x_{6}}\left({1-{t_{6}}}\right)}}

{t_{4}}={\left[{C,A;E,F}\right]_{D}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
{z_{6}}\\
{t_{6}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle{y_{6}}\atop{\scriptstyle 1\atop\scriptstyle{y_{6}}}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&0&{{y_{6}}}\\
1&0&1\\
0&1&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1&{{y_{6}}}\\
0&{{z_{6}}}&1\\
0&{{t_{6}}}&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&{{y_{6}}}\\
1&{{z_{6}}}&1\\
0&{{t_{6}}}&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0&{{y_{6}}}\\
0&0&1\\
0&1&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{y_{6}}\left({{y_{6}}{z_{6}}-{t_{6}}}\right)}}{{{y_{6}}\left({1-{t_{6}}}\right)}}=\frac{{{y_{6}}{z_{6}}-{t_{6}}}}{{1-{t_{6}}}}

After getting rid of the denominators, and omitting negligible terms, we get:

\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\approx 0\\
{x_{4}}\approx{z_{4}}{y_{4}}\\
{z_{2}}={x_{4}}\\
{t_{2}}{y_{4}}=1\\
{y_{2}}\approx{y_{6}}\\
0\approx{x_{6}}\\
{t_{2}}{x_{6}}\approx{t_{6}}\\
{t_{4}}\approx{y_{6}}{z_{6}}-{t_{6}}\end{array}

Recall that
t_{2},y_{4},z_{6}
are non-zero, while other coordinates are near zero.
The differential versions of these are

\begin{array}[]{c}d{x_{2}}=0\\
d{x_{4}}={y_{4}}\cdot d{z_{4}}\\
d{z_{2}}=d{x_{4}}\\
{t_{2}}d{y_{4}}+{y_{4}}d{t_{2}}=0\\
d{y_{2}}=d{y_{6}}\\
0=d{x_{6}}\\
{t_{2}}d{x_{6}}=d{t_{6}}\\
d{t_{4}}={z_{6}}d{y_{6}}-d{t_{6}}\end{array}

From the first, the sixth, and the seventh equation we see that
dx_{2},dx_{6},dt_{6}
are zeroes.
From
dz_{2},dx_{4}
can be expressed can be expressed from the third and the second equations
by
dz_{4}
. So cotangent space is spanned by

dy_{2},dt_{2},dy_{4},dz_{4},dt_{4},dy_{6},dz_{6},

with relations

\begin{array}[]{c}{t_{2}}d{y_{4}}+{y_{4}}d{t_{2}}=0\\
d{y_{2}}=d{y_{6}}\\
d{t_{4}}={z_{6}}d{y_{6}}\end{array}

The last two of these three equations allow to express
dt_{4}
and
dy_{6}
in terms of
dy_{2}
.
The first equations allows to express
dt_{2}
from
dy_{4}
. So the cotangent space is spanned by

dy_{2},dy_{4},dz_{4},dz_{6}
hence it is at most four-dimensional.

5.10
\mathcal{E}.2

The points were renumbered to emphasize the symmetry.

We shall not discuss the charts 4-6, as it is obvious all that the first three charts contain all the cross ratios and define the last charts completely.
On the first three charts, we choose the following coordinates:

\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{1}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{1}}=\left({{y_{1}}:1:{x_{1}}}\right)\\
{C_{1}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{D_{1}}=\left({0:1:1}\right)\\
{E_{1}}=\left({{z_{1}}:{z_{1}}{t_{1}}:1}\right)\\
{F_{1}}=\left({1:1:0}\right)\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{2}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{2}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{C_{2}}=\left({{x_{2}}:{y_{2}}:1}\right)\\
{D_{2}}=\left({0:1:1}\right)\\
{E_{2}}=\left({1:0:1}\right)\\
{F_{2}}=\left({1:{z_{2}}:{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}\right)\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{3}}=\left({1:{x_{3}}:{y_{3}}}\right)\\
{B_{3}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{C_{3}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{D_{3}}=\left({{z_{3}}{t_{3}}:1:{z_{3}}}\right)\\
{E_{3}}=\left({1:0:1}\right)\\
{F_{3}}=\left({1:1:0}\right)\end{array}\end{array}

Notice that since the first three charts are completely degenerate, in the neighborhood of this point

{x_{1}},{y_{1}},{z_{1}},{t_{1}},{x_{2}},{y_{2}},{z_{2}},{t_{2}},{x_{3}},{y_{3}},{z_{3}},{t_{3}}\sim 0.

For the last three charts we also choose coordinates (we assume that the extra lines remain the medians of the triangle
P_{1}P_{2}P_{3}
):

\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{7}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{7}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{C_{7}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{D_{7}}=\left({{y_{7}}:1:{x_{7}}}\right)\\
{E_{7}}=\left({{z_{7}}:{z_{7}}:1}\right)\\
{F_{7}}=\left({{t_{7}}:1:{t_{7}}}\right)\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{8}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{8}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{C_{8}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{D_{8}}=\left({{t_{8}}:{t_{8}}:1}\right)\\
{E_{8}}=\left({{x_{8}}:{y_{8}}:1}\right)\\
{F_{8}}=\left({1:{z_{8}}:{z_{8}}}\right)\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{9}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{9}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{C_{9}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{D_{9}}=\left({{z_{9}}:1:{z_{9}}}\right)\\
{E_{9}}=\left({1:{t_{9}}:{t_{9}}}\right)\\
{F_{9}}=\left({1:{x_{9}}:{y_{9}}}\right)\end{array}\end{array}

Here
{y_{7}},{z_{7}},{t_{7}},{y_{8}},{z_{8}},{t_{8}},{y_{9}},{z_{9}},{t_{9}}\sim 0
.
The coordinates were chosen in a symmetric way.

From the first chart we may see that

{x_{1}}={\left[{F,C;B,D}\right]_{A}}

{y_{1}}={\left[{D,A;B,F}\right]_{C}}

The third coordinate
z_{1}
is not a cross ratio, but we can relate it by

{\left[{C,A;E,F}\right]_{D}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{z_{1}}}&0\\
0&{{z_{1}}{t_{1}}}&1\\
1&1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1&0\\
0&1&1\\
0&0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{z_{1}}}&0\\
0&{{z_{1}}{t_{1}}}&1\\
0&1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&0\\
0&1&1\\
1&0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{z_{1}}}}{{{z_{1}}{t_{1}}-1}}\approx-z_{1}

Since
z_{1}t_{1}\approx 0
as
z_{1},t_{1}\sim 0
.
Lastly,

{t_{1}}={\left[{A,D;E,F}\right]_{C}}

We may relate these formulas to the coordinate of the seventh chart:

{x_{1}}={\left[{F,C;B,D}\right]_{A}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{{t_{7}}}&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&{{x_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{{t_{7}}}&{{x_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{t_{7}}}}{{{t_{7}}-{x_{7}}}}

{y_{1}}={\left[{D,A;B,F}\right]_{C}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{y_{7}}}&0\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{t_{7}}}\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{y_{7}}}&{{t_{7}}}\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{y_{7}}}}{{{y_{7}}-{t_{7}}}}

-{z_{1}}\approx{\left[{C,A;E,F}\right]_{D}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{z_{7}}}&{{y_{7}}}\\
0&{{z_{7}}}&1\\
1&1&{{x_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{t_{7}}}&{{y_{7}}}\\
0&1&1\\
0&{{t_{7}}}&{{x_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{t_{7}}}&{{y_{7}}}\\
0&1&1\\
1&{{t_{7}}}&{{x_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{z_{7}}}&{{y_{7}}}\\
0&{{z_{7}}}&1\\
0&1&{{x_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=

=\frac{{{z_{7}}\left({1-{y_{7}}}\right)\left({{x_{7}}-{t_{7}}}\right)}}{{\left({{t_{7}}-{y_{7}}}\right)\left({{z_{7}}{x_{7}}-1}\right)}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{t_{1}}={\left[{A,D;E,F}\right]_{C}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{z_{7}}}\\
0&{{z_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{y_{7}}}&{{t_{7}}}\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{t_{7}}}\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{y_{7}}}&{{z_{7}}}\\
1&{{z_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left({{y_{7}}-{t_{7}}}\right)}}{{1\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{y_{7}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{{y_{7}}-{t_{7}}}}{{{y_{7}}-1}}

Notice also that

{z_{7}}={\left[{C,A;E,F}\right]_{B}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
{x_{3}}\\
{y_{3}}\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 1\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{{y_{3}}}&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{{y_{3}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{-{y_{3}}}}{{1-{y_{3}}}}

Multiplying by the denominators and
recalling
{x_{1}},{y_{1}},{z_{1}},{t_{1}},{y_{7}},{z_{7}},{t_{7}}\sim 0
we get

\begin{array}[]{l}-{x_{1}}{x_{7}}\approx{t_{7}}\\
0\approx{y_{7}}\\
0\approx z_{7}x_{7}\\
-t_{1}\approx y_{7}-t_{7}\\
z_{7}\approx-y_{3}.\end{array}

Hence

\begin{array}[]{l}-{x_{7}}\cdot d{x_{1}}=d{t_{7}}\\
0=d{y_{7}}\\
0={x_{7}}\cdot d{z_{7}}\\
-d{t_{1}}=d{y_{7}}-d{t_{7}}\\
dz_{7}=-dy_{3}.\end{array}

Using the second of these four equations, the fourth equation may be rewritten as

d{t_{1}}=d{t_{7}}

Since our coordinates were chosen symmetrically, we may similarly relate charts 2 and 8, and also charts 3 and 9.
Hence

\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{l}-{x_{7}}\cdot d{x_{1}}=d{t_{7}}\\
0=d{y_{7}}\\
0={x_{7}}\cdot d{z_{7}}\\
d{t_{1}}=d{t_{7}}\\
dz_{7}=-dy_{3}\end{array}&\begin{array}[]{l}-{x_{8}}\cdot d{x_{2}}=d{t_{8}}\\
0=d{y_{8}}\\
0={x_{8}}\cdot d{z_{8}}\\
d{t_{2}}=d{t_{8}}\\
dz_{8}=-dy_{1}\end{array}&\begin{array}[]{l}-{x_{9}}\cdot d{x_{3}}=d{t_{9}}\\
0=d{y_{9}}\\
0={x_{9}}\cdot d{z_{9}}\\
d{t_{3}}=d{t_{9}}\\
dz_{9}=-dy_{2}\end{array}\end{array}

Now we connect charts 1 and 2.

{x_{1}}={\left[{F,C;B,D}\right]_{A}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{{t_{2}}}&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{y_{2}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{{t_{2}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{y_{2}}}&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{t_{2}}\left({{y_{2}}-1}\right)}}{{1-{t_{2}}}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{y_{1}}={\left[{D,A;B,F}\right]_{C}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&0&{{x_{2}}}\\
1&1&{{y_{2}}}\\
1&0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1&{{x_{2}}}\\
0&{{z_{2}}}&{{y_{2}}}\\
0&{{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&{{x_{2}}}\\
1&{{z_{2}}}&{{y_{2}}}\\
1&{{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0&{{x_{2}}}\\
0&1&{{y_{2}}}\\
0&0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}\end{array}

=\frac{{-{x_{2}}\cdot{z_{2}}\cdot\left({1-{y_{2}}{t_{2}}}\right)}}{{-1+{y_{2}}-{x_{2}}{z_{2}}+{x_{2}}{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}}\approx 0

\begin{array}[]{l}-{z_{1}}\approx{\left[{C,A;E,F}\right]_{D}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&1&0\\
{{y_{2}}}&0&1\\
1&1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1&0\\
0&{{z_{2}}}&1\\
0&{{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&1&0\\
{{y_{2}}}&{{z_{2}}}&1\\
1&{{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1&0\\
0&0&1\\
0&1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\end{array}

=-\frac{{\left({1-{y_{2}}-{x_{2}}}\right)\cdot{z_{2}}\left({1-{t_{2}}}\right)}}{{1-{y_{2}}+{x_{2}}{z_{2}}\left({1-{t_{2}}}\right)}}

We could also write the equation for
t_{1}
, but we would not conclude new information from it.
After multiplying by denominators and recalling
x_{i},y_{i},z_{i},t_{i}\sim 0
for
i\in{1,2}
we see that

\begin{array}[]{l}{x_{1}}=-{z_{2}}\\
{y_{1}}\approx 0\\
{z_{1}}\approx{z_{2}}\\
\end{array}

Because of cyclic symmetry, similar formulas connect the second chart to the third, and the third to the first.
So together with the former set of formulas and using the symmetry, we get the following set of relations in the cotangent space:

\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{l}-{x_{7}}\cdot d{x_{1}}=d{t_{7}}\\
0=d{y_{7}}\\
0={x_{7}}\cdot d{z_{7}}\\
d{t_{1}}=d{t_{7}}\\
dz_{7}=-dy_{3}\end{array}&\begin{array}[]{l}-{x_{8}}\cdot d{x_{2}}=d{t_{8}}\\
0=d{y_{8}}\\
0={x_{8}}\cdot d{z_{8}}\\
d{t_{2}}=d{t_{8}}\\
dz_{8}=-dy_{1}\end{array}&\begin{array}[]{l}-{x_{9}}\cdot d{x_{3}}=d{t_{9}}\\
0=d{y_{9}}\\
0={x_{9}}\cdot d{z_{9}}\\
d{t_{3}}=d{t_{9}}\\
dz_{9}=-dy_{2}\end{array}\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{l}d{x_{1}}=-d{z_{2}}\\
d{y_{1}}=0\\
d{z_{1}}=d{z_{2}}\end{array}&\begin{array}[]{l}d{x_{2}}=-d{z_{3}}\\
d{y_{2}}=0\\
d{z_{2}}=d{z_{3}}\end{array}&\begin{array}[]{l}d{x_{3}}=-d{z_{1}}\\
d{y_{3}}=0\\
d{z_{3}}=d{z_{1}}\end{array}\end{array}

The cotangent space is spanned by
x_{i},y_{i},z_{i},t_{i}
, where
i\in\left\{1,2,3,7,8,9\right\}
.
Anyway,
dy_{i}=0
because of the formulas in the second and the seventh lines; it also allows
to simplify the fifth line, which makes the third line redundant.
From the fourth line we may express
dt_{1},dt_{2},dt_{2}
, and from the first line we may may express

dt_{7},dt_{8},dt_{9}
. So we may remain with
x_{i},z_{i}
and relations:

\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{l}d{z_{7}}=0\\
d{x_{1}}=-d{z_{2}}\\
d{z_{1}}=d{z_{2}}\end{array}&\begin{array}[]{l}d{z_{8}}=0\\
d{x_{2}}=-d{z_{3}}\\
d{z_{2}}=d{z_{3}}\end{array}&\begin{array}[]{l}d{z_{9}}=0\\
d{x_{3}}=-d{z_{1}}\\
d{z_{3}}=d{z_{1}}\end{array}\end{array}

From the last line we see that
dz_{1}=dz_{2}=dz_{3}
, so we may forget
dz_{2}
and
dz_{3}
and replace them by
dz_{1}
.
From the line before the last we conclude that
dx_{1}=dx_{2}=dx_{3}=-dz_{1}
, so we may forget
dx_{1},dx_{2},dx_{3}
and replace them everywhere by
-dz_{1}
. From the first line we see that
dz_{7}=dz_{8}=dz_{9}
.
So the cotangent space is spanned by
dz_{1}
and
dx_{7},dx_{8},dx_{9}
, and is at most four-dimensional.

5.11
\mathcal{E}.3

We choose the coordinates related to the seven charts:

\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{1}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{1}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{C_{1}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{D_{1}}=\left({{x_{1}}{y_{1}}:1:{x_{1}}}\right)\\
{E_{1}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{F_{1}}=\left({{z_{1}}{t_{1}}:1:{z_{1}}}\right)\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{2}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{2}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{C_{2}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{D_{2}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{E_{2}}=\left({{x_{2}}:1+{y_{2}}:1}\right)\\
{F_{2}}=\left({{z_{2}}{t_{2}}:1:{z_{2}}}\right)\end{array}\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{3}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{3}}=\left({1:{x_{3}}:{x_{3}}{y_{3}}}\right)\\
{C_{3}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{D_{3}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{E_{3}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{F_{3}}=\left({1:{z_{3}}{t_{3}}:{z_{3}}}\right)\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{4}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{4}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{C_{4}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{D_{4}}=\left({1+{x_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}:{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}:1}\right)\\
{E_{4}}=\left({1+{x_{4}}:{y_{4}}:1}\right)\\
{F_{4}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\end{array}\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{5}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{5}}=\left({1:{x_{5}}:{x_{5}}{y_{5}}}\right)\\
{C_{5}}=\left({{z_{5}}:1+{t_{5}}:1}\right)\\
{D_{5}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{E_{5}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{F_{5}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{6}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{6}}=\left({1:{x_{6}}:{x_{6}}{y_{6}}}\right)\\
{C_{6}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{D_{6}}=\left({{z_{6}}{t_{6}}:1:{z_{6}}}\right)\\
{E_{6}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{F_{6}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{7}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{B_{7}}=\left({{z_{7}}:1:{z_{7}}}\right)\\
{C_{7}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{D_{7}}=\left({{t_{7}}:{t_{7}}:1}\right)\\
{E_{7}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{F_{7}}=\left({{y_{7}}:1:{x_{7}}}\right)\end{array}\end{array}

These charts, except for the last chart, are most degenerate (having no parameters).
So we have chosen the coordinates so that all of them are zero at this configuration
and near zero in the neighborhood. In the last chart the only parameter
that can be non-zero is
x_{7}
.

Firstly, we shall get rid of the first chart:

\begin{array}[]{l}{x_{1}}={\left[{C,E;D,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\
1+{y_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{y_{2}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{y_{2}}}&0\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{y_{2}}}}{{1+{y_{2}}}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{y_{1}}={\left[{E,A;D,B}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\
1+{y_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 1\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&0\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{x_{2}}}}{{{x_{2}}-1}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{z_{1}}={\left[{C,E;F,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\
1+{y_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{2}}{t_{2}}\\
1\\
{z_{2}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&{{z_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{y_{2}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{y_{2}}}&1\\
1&{{z_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{z_{2}}\cdot{y_{2}}}}{{{z_{2}}\left({1+{y_{2}}}\right)-1}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{t_{1}}={\left[{E,A;F,B}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\
1+{y_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{2}}{t_{2}}\\
1\\
{z_{2}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 1\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&{{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}\\
1&{{z_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}\\
0&{{z_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&{{t_{2}}}\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{t_{2}}-{x_{2}}}}{{1-{x_{2}}}}

Since all denominators are distinct from zero,
dx_{1},dy_{1},dz_{1},dt_{1}
can be expressed
using coordinates related to the second chart.

Secondly, we get rid of the third chart:

\begin{array}[]{l}{x_{3}}={\left[{A,E;B,D}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\

0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\

1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle{z_{5}}\atop{\scriptstyle 1+{t_{5}}\atop\scriptstyle 1}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1&{{z_{5}}}\\
0&{{x_{5}}}&{1+{t_{5}}}\\
0&{{x_{5}}{y_{5}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&0&{{z_{5}}}\\

0&1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0&{{z_{5}}}\\

1&{{x_{5}}}&{1+{t_{5}}}\\
0&{{x_{5}}{y_{5}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{x_{5}}\left({1-{y_{5}}\left({1+{t_{5}}}\right)}\right)\cdot{z_{5}}}}{{\left({1+{t_{5}}}\right)\left({1-{x_{5}}{y_{5}}{z_{5}}}\right)}}

\begin{array}[]{l}{y_{3}}={\left[{E,C;B,D}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&x_{5}\\

\begin{array}[]{l}{z_{3}}={\left[{A,C;F,D}\right]_{E}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\

\begin{array}[]{l}{t_{3}}={\left[{C,E;F,D}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{5}}\\

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{t_{5}}}&1\\

Again, all denominators are non-zero,
dx_{3},dy_{3},dz_{3},dt_{3}
can be expressed
using coordinates related to the fifth chart.

\begin{array}[]{l}{x_{6}}={\left[{A,E;B,F}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\

\begin{array}[]{l}{y_{6}}={\left[{E,C;B,F}\right]_{A}}={\left[\small{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1+{x_{4}}\\

\begin{array}[]{l}{t_{6}}={\left[{C,A;D,F}\right]_{E}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{5}}\\

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{z_{5}}}&0\\

0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{z_{5}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\

Again, all denominators are non-zero,
dx_{6},dy_{6},dz_{6},dt_{6}
can be expressed
using coordinates related to the fourth and the fifth chart.
Only 4 charts remain: the second, the fourth, the fifth, and the seventh.
Now we shall get rid of the second chart:

\begin{array}[]{l}{x_{2}}={\left[{D,A;E,B}\right]_{C}}={\left[\scriptsize{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1+{x_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}\\
{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\

0\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 1\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}&{1+{x_{4}}}\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}&0\\

0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}}{{1+{x_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}}\approx 0

\begin{array}[]{l}1+{y_{2}}={\left[{D,C;E,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[\footnotesize{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{5}}\\
1+{t_{5}}\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
