0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
{x_{5}}\\
{x_{5}}{y_{5}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\\
=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{t_{5}}}&{{x_{5}}}\\
1&{{x_{5}}{y_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{x_{5}}}\\
1&{{x_{5}}{y_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{t_{5}}}&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{t_{5}}}&1\\
1&{{y_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&{{y_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}\approx 1-{y_{5}}\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{l}{z_{2}}={\left[{C,D;F,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1+{x_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}\\
{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}}&0\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}}}{{{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}-1}}\approx-(y_{4}+z_{4})

\begin{array}[]{l}{t_{2}}={\left[{D,A;F,B}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1+{x_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}\\
{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 1\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}&0\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{x_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}}{{1+{x_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}}\approx x_{4}

We see that
dx_{2},dy_{2},dz_{2},dt_{2}
are easily expressed in terms of coordinates related to the
charts 4 and 5. So we remain with 3 charts: the fourth, the fifth and the seventh.
We connect the fifth chart to the fourth:

\begin{array}[]{l}{x_{5}}={\left[{A,E;B,F}\right]_{D}}={\left[\footnotesize{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1+{x_{4}}\\
{y_{4}}\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1+{x_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}\atop{\scriptstyle{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}\atop\scriptstyle 1}}\right)}}=\end{array}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0&{1+{x_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}\\
0&0&{{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}}\\
0&1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{4}}}&1&{1+{x_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}\\
{{y_{4}}}&1&{{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}}\\
1&1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1&{1+{x_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}\\
0&1&{{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}}\\
0&1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{4}}}&0&{1+{x_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}\\
{{y_{4}}}&0&{{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}}\\
1&1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=

=\frac{{-\left({{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}}\right)\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{4}}}&1&{{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}\\
{{y_{4}}}&1&{{z_{4}}}\\
1&1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left({1-\left({{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}}\right)}\right)\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{4}}}&0&{{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}\\
{{y_{4}}}&0&{{z_{4}}}\\
1&1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=

=\frac{{-\left({{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}}\right)\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{4}}}&0&{{t_{4}}}\\
{{y_{4}}}&1&1\\
1&1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left({1-\left({{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}}\right)}\right)\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{4}}}&0&{{t_{4}}}\\
{{y_{4}}}&0&1\\
1&1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=

=\frac{{\left({{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}}\right)\cdot\left({{x_{4}}+{t_{4}}-{t_{4}}{y_{4}}}\right)}}{{\left({1-\left({{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}}\right)}\right)\cdot\left({{y_{4}}{t_{4}}-1-{x_{4}}}\right)}}\approx 0

{y_{5}}={\left[{E,D;B,F}\right]_{A}}={\left[\footnotesize{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1+{x_{4}}\\
{y_{4}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1+{x_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}\\
{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{y_{4}}}&0\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{y_{4}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}}&0\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{y_{4}}\left({{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}-1}\right)}}{{\left({{y_{4}}-1}\right)\left({{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}}\right)}}

{z_{5}}={\left[{D,A;C,F}\right]_{E}}={\left[\footnotesize{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1+{x_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}\\
{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1+{x_{4}}\atop{\scriptstyle{y_{4}}\atop\scriptstyle 1}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}&0&{1+{x_{4}}}\\
{{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}}&1&{{y_{4}}}\\
1&0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1&{1+{x_{4}}}\\
0&1&{{y_{4}}}\\
0&1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}&1&{1+{x_{4}}}\\
{{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}}&1&{{y_{4}}}\\
1&1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0&{1+{x_{4}}}\\
0&1&{{y_{4}}}\\
0&0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}&0&{1+{x_{4}}}\\
{{z_{4}}}&1&{{y_{4}}}\\
0&0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left({1-{y_{4}}}\right)}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}&1&{1+{x_{4}}}\\
{{z_{4}}}&1&{{y_{4}}}\\
0&1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{t_{4}}}&0&{1+{x_{4}}}\\
1&1&{{y_{4}}}\\
0&0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left({1-{y_{4}}}\right)}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{t_{4}}}&0&{{x_{4}}}\\
1&1&{{y_{4}}}\\
0&1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=

=\frac{{{t_{4}}\left({1-{y_{4}}}\right)}}{{{t_{4}}-{t_{4}}{y_{4}}+{x_{4}}}}

1+{t_{5}}={\left[{D,E;C,F}\right]_{A}}=\scriptsize{\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1+{x_{4}}+{z_{4}}{t_{4}}\\
{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1+{x_{4}}\\
{y_{4}}\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}=}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}}&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{y_{4}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{y_{4}}}&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{1-{y_{4}}}}{{{1-\left({{y_{4}}+{z_{4}}}\right)}}}

After getting rid of the denominators and omitting the negligible terms we get:

\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{5}}\approx 0\\
0\approx-{y_{4}}\\
0\approx{t_{4}}\\
1+{t_{5}}\approx 1+{z_{4}}\end{array}

Which means

\begin{array}[]{c}d{x_{5}}=0\\
d{y_{4}}=0\\
d{t_{4}}=0\\
d{t_{5}}=d{z_{4}}\end{array}

To connect to the seventh chart we write:

{x_{5}}={\left[{A,E;B,F}\right]_{D}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{z_{7}}}&{{t_{7}}}\\
1&1&{{t_{7}}}\\
0&{{z_{7}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{y_{7}}}&{{t_{7}}}\\
0&1&{{t_{7}}}\\
0&{{x_{7}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{y_{7}}}&{{t_{7}}}\\
1&1&{{t_{7}}}\\
0&{{x_{7}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{z_{7}}}&{{t_{7}}}\\
0&1&{{t_{7}}}\\
0&{{z_{7}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=

=\frac{{{z_{7}}\left({{t_{7}}-1}\right)\left({1-{x_{7}}{t_{7}}}\right)}}{{\left({{x_{7}}{t_{7}}-{y_{7}}}\right)\left({1-{z_{7}}{t_{7}}}\right)}}

{z_{5}}={\left[{D,A;C,F}\right]_{E}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{t_{7}}}&0\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&{{x_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{t_{7}}}&1\\
1&{{x_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{x_{7}}{t_{7}}}}{{{x_{7}}{t_{7}}-1}}

1+{t_{5}}={\left[{D,E;C,F}\right]_{A}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{t_{7}}}&0\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{y_{7}}}\\
0&{{x_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{t_{7}}}&{{y_{7}}}\\
1&{{x_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{t_{7}}{x_{7}}}}{{{t_{7}}{x_{7}}-{y_{7}}}}

{t_{7}}={\left[{C,E;D,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{5}}\\
1+{t_{5}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
{x_{5}}\\
{x_{5}}{y_{5}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=

=\small\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{t_{5}}}&0\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{x_{5}}}\\
0&{{x_{5}}{y_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{t_{5}}}&{{x_{5}}}\\
1&{{x_{5}}{y_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{\left({1+{t_{5}}}\right)\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&{{y_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{t_{5}}}&1\\
1&{{y_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{\left({1+{t_{5}}}\right){y_{5}}}}{{\left({1+{t_{5}}}\right){y_{5}}-1}}

From the last 4 formulas we conclude (after multiplying by the denominators and skipping the negligible terms)

\begin{array}[]{l}0\approx-{z_{7}}\\
{z_{5}}\approx{x_{7}}{t_{7}}\\
{t_{7}}{x_{7}}-{y_{7}}\approx{t_{7}}{x_{7}}\\
-t_{7}\approx y_{5}\end{array}

Hence

\begin{array}[]{l}d{z_{7}}=0\\
d{z_{5}}={x_{7}}\cdot d{t_{7}}\\
d{y_{7}}=0\\
-dt_{7}=dy_{5}\end{array}

Previously, we had four more equalities:

\begin{array}[]{c}d{x_{5}}=d{y_{4}}=d{t_{4}}=0\\
d{t_{5}}=d{z_{4}}\end{array}

So, out of
dx_{i},dy_{i},dz_{i},dt_{i}
for
i\in\left\{4,5,7\right\}
,
five are zeroes, so the cotangent space is spanned by
dx_{4},dz_{4},dy_{5},dz_{5},dt_{5},dx_{7},dt_{7}

with relations

\begin{array}[]{l}d{z_{5}}={x_{7}}\cdot d{t_{7}}\\
-dt_{7}=dy_{5}\\
d{t_{5}}=d{z_{4}}\end{array}

which express all the remaining coordinates from the fifth chart from the other charts,
so the cotangent space is spanned by
dx_{4},dz_{4},dx_{7},dt_{7}
.

5.12
\mathcal{E}.6

\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{1}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{1}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{C_{1}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{D_{1}}=\left({{x_{1}}{y_{1}}:{x_{1}}:1}\right)\\
{E_{1}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{F_{1}}=\left({{z_{1}}{t_{1}}:{z_{1}}:1}\right)\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{2}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{2}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{C_{2}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{D_{2}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{E_{2}}=\left({{x_{2}}:1+{y_{2}}:1}\right)\\
{F_{2}}=\left({{z_{2}}{t_{2}}:{z_{2}}:1}\right)\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{3}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{3}}=\left({1:{x_{3}}:{x_{3}}{y_{3}}}\right)\\
{C_{3}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{D_{3}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{E_{3}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{F_{3}}=\left({{z_{3}}:{z_{3}}{t_{3}}:1}\right)\end{array}\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{4}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{4}}=\left({1:{x_{4}}:{x_{4}}{y_{4}}}\right)\\
{C_{4}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{D_{4}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{E_{4}}=\left({{z_{4}}:1:{z_{4}}{t_{4}}}\right)\\
{F_{4}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{5}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{5}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{C_{5}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{D_{5}}=\left({1:1+{x_{5}}:{y_{5}}}\right)\\
{E_{5}}=\left({1:1+{x_{5}}+{z_{5}}}\right.\\
\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\left.{:{y_{5}}+{z_{5}}{t_{5}}}\right)\\
{F_{5}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{6}}=\left({1:1+{x_{6}}:{y_{6}}}\right)\\
{B_{6}}=\left({1:1+{x_{6}}+{z_{6}}}\right.\\
\left.{\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,:{y_{6}}+{z_{6}}{t_{6}}}\right)\\
{C_{6}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{D_{6}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{E_{6}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{F_{6}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\end{array}\end{array}

The only coordinate which may be far from zero is
t_{5}
, which governs
the direction of the line via the double point.

We start by eliminating the coordinates of the first chart:

{x_{1}}={\left[{C,E;D,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\

1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{y_{2}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{y_{2}}}&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=-{y_{2}}

{y_{1}}={\left[{E,A;D,B}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\
1+{y_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 1}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&0\\
{1+{y_{2}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&1\\
{1+{y_{2}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{x_{2}}}}{{{x_{2}}-{y_{2}}-1}}

{z_{1}}={\left[{C,E;F,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\
1+{y_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{2}}{t_{2}}\\
{z_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{z_{2}}}\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{y_{2}}}&1\\

1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{y_{2}}}&{{z_{2}}}\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{z_{2}}\cdot{y_{2}}}}{{1+{y_{2}}-{z_{2}}}}

{t_{1}}={\left[{E,A;F,B}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\

{z_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\

1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 1}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&{{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}\\
{1+{y_{2}}}&{{z_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&1\\
{1+{y_{2}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}\\
0&{{z_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{x_{2}}-\left({1+{y_{2}}}\right){t_{2}}}}{{{x_{2}}-1-{y_{2}}}}

All denominators are non-zero, so the coordinates of the first chart are not needed.
Now similarly for the third chart:

{x_{3}}={\left[{A,E;B,D}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\

{1+{y_{2}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&1\\
{1+{y_{2}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{x_{2}}}}{{{x_{2}}-1-{y_{2}}}}

{y_{3}}={\left[{E,C;B,D}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{y_{2}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{y_{2}}}&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\

{z_{3}}={\left[{C,A;F,D}\right]_{E}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\

{{y_{6}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{y_{6}}}}{{{y_{6}}-1-{x_{6}}}}

{t_{3}}={\left[{A,E;F,D}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{z_{4}}}&0\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{z_{4}}}&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{z_{4}}}}{{{z_{4}}-1}}

Again, all denominators are non-zero, so the third chart might also be omitted.
Now, the fourth chart:

{x_{4}}={\left[{A,D;B,F}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\

1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{t_{2}}}\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}={t_{2}}

{y_{4}}={\left[{D,C;B,F}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\

{z_{2}}\\

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\

{z_{4}}={\left[{D,A;E,F}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\

{1+{x_{6}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\

{1+{x_{6}}}&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{x_{6}}}}{{1+{x_{6}}}}

{t_{4}}={\left[{A,C;E,F}\right]_{D}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{{y_{6}}}&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{{y_{6}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{-{y_{6}}}}{{1-{y_{6}}}}

And here again, all denominators are non-zero.

We remain with just 3 charts: the second, the fifth, and the sixth.

{t_{2}}={\left[{D,A;F,B}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\

{z_{2}}={\left[{C,D;F,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\

{{y_{5}}}&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\

{{y_{5}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{y_{5}}}}{{{y_{5}}-1-{x_{5}}}}\approx-{y_{5}}

On the other hand,

{z_{2}}={\left[{C,D;F,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[\small{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&1\\
0&1&{1+{x_{6}}}\\
1&1&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&1\\
1&{1+{x_{6}}+{z_{6}}}&{1+{x_{6}}}\\

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\

{{z_{6}}{t_{6}}}&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
{{z_{6}}}&{1+{x_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
1&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{x_{6}}{t_{6}}}}{{{y_{6}}-1}}\approx 0

t_{2}={\left[{D,A;F,B}\right]_{C}}={\left[\footnotesize{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{1+{x_{6}}}&{1+{x_{6}}+{z_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&{1+{x_{6}}+{z_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{1+{x_{6}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
{1+{x_{6}}}&{{z_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{{x_{6}}}&0\end{array}}\right)}}=-\frac{{{z_{6}}}}{{{x_{6}}}}

Now we shall compute
{\left[{A,E;B,D}\right]_{C}}
in all the remaining charts.

{\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{1+{x_{6}}}&{1+{x_{6}}+{z_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
{1+{x_{6}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&{1+{x_{6}}+{z_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{z_{6}}}}{{1+{x_{6}}+{z_{6}}}}\approx{z_{6}}

{\left[{A,E;B,D}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\
1+{y_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 1}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&0\\
{1+{y_{2}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&1\\
{1+{y_{2}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{x_{2}}}}{{{x_{2}}-1-{y_{2}}}}\approx-{x_{2}}

{\left[{A,E;B,D}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1+{x_{5}}+{z_{5}}\\
{y_{5}}+{z_{5}}{t_{5}}\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1+{x_{5}}\\
{y_{5}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 1}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{1+{x_{5}}+{z_{5}}}&{1+{x_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&{1+{x_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
{1+{x_{5}}+{z_{5}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\small\frac{{\left|{\left(\small{\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
{{z_{5}}}&{1+{x_{5}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{1+{x_{5}}}}=

=\frac{{-{z_{5}}}}{{1+{x_{5}}}}\approx{-z_{5}}

Therefore
{z_{6}}\approx-{x_{2}}\approx-{z_{5}}
.

1+{y_{2}}={\left[{C,D;E,B}\right]_{A}}={\footnotesize\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1+{x_{6}}+{z_{6}}\\
{y_{6}}+{z_{6}}{t_{6}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 1+{x_{6}}\atop\scriptstyle{y_{6}}}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&1\\
0&0&{1+{x_{6}}}\\
1&0&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&1\\
1&{1+{x_{6}}+{z_{6}}}&{1+{x_{6}}}\\
0&{{y_{6}}+{z_{6}}{t_{6}}}&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&1\\
0&{1+{x_{6}}+{z_{6}}}&{1+{x_{6}}}\\
1&{{y_{6}}+{z_{6}}{t_{6}}}&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&1\\
1&0&{1+{x_{6}}}\\
0&0&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=

=\frac{{\left({1+{x_{6}}}\right)\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{{y_{6}}+{z_{6}}{t_{6}}}&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{1+{x_{6}}+{z_{6}}}&{1+{x_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot{y_{6}}}}=\frac{{\left({1+{x_{6}}}\right)\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
{{z_{6}}{t_{6}}}&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
{{z_{6}}}&{1+{x_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot{y_{6}}}}=\frac{{\left({1+{x_{6}}}\right){t_{6}}}}{{{y_{6}}}}

In other words
y_{6}\approx t_{6}
.

Now we compute
{\left[{B,E;C,D}\right]_{A}}
in the coordinates of the second and the sixth charts:

{\left[{B,E;C,D}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\\
1+{y_{2}}\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&1\\
{1+{y_{2}}}&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{x_{2}}}&0\\
{1+{y_{2}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{1+{y_{2}}}}{{{x_{2}}}}

{\left[{B,E;C,D}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1+{x_{6}}+{z_{6}}\\
{y_{6}}+{z_{6}}{t_{6}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 1+{x_{6}}\atop\scriptstyle{y_{6}}}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0&1\\
{1+{x_{6}}+{z_{6}}}&0&{1+{x_{6}}}\\
{{y_{6}}+{z_{6}}{t_{6}}}&1&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0&1\\
0&1&{1+{x_{6}}}\\
0&0&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0&1\\
{1+{x_{6}}+{z_{6}}}&1&{1+{x_{6}}}\\
{{y_{6}}+{z_{6}}{t_{6}}}&0&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0&1\\
0&0&{1+{x_{6}}}\\
0&1&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&0&1\\
{{z_{6}}}&0&{1+{x_{6}}}\\
{{z_{6}}{t_{6}}}&1&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot{y_{6}}}}{{-\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&0&1\\
{{z_{6}}}&1&{1+{x_{6}}}\\
{{z_{6}}{t_{6}}}&0&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left({1+{x_{6}}}\right)}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
{{z_{6}}}&{1+{x_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot{y_{6}}}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
{{z_{6}}{t_{6}}}&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left({1+{x_{6}}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&{1+{x_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot{y_{6}}}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
{{t_{6}}}&{{y_{6}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left({1+{x_{6}}}\right)}}=\frac{{{y_{6}}}}{{{t_{6}}\left({1+{x_{6}}}\right)}}

Therefore:

\frac{{{y_{6}}}}{{{t_{6}}\left({1+{x_{6}}}\right)}}=\frac{{1+{y_{2}}}}{{{x_{2}}}}

0\approx{y_{6}}{x_{2}}={t_{6}}\left({1+{x_{6}}}\right)\left({1+{y_{2}}}\right)\approx{t_{6}}

To summarize:

\begin{array}[]{c}{t_{2}}=-{x_{5}}\\
-{y_{5}}\approx{z_{2}}\approx 0\\
0\approx-{x_{6}}{t_{2}}\approx{z_{6}}\\
{z_{6}}\approx-{x_{2}}\approx-{z_{5}}\\
{y_{6}}\approx{t_{6}}\\
{t_{6}}\approx 0\end{array}

After simplification:

\begin{array}[]{c}{t_{2}}=-{x_{5}}\\
{y_{5}}\approx{z_{2}}\approx 0\approx{z_{6}}\approx{x_{2}}\approx{z_{5}}\\
{y_{6}}\approx{t_{6}}\approx 0\end{array}

Hence

dt_{2}=-dx_{5}

0=dy_{5}=dz_{2}=dz_{6}=dx_{2}=dz_{5}=dy_{6}=dt_{6}

Therefore
dx_{2},dz_{2},dy_{5},dz_{5},dy_{6},dz_{6},dt_{6}
are zeroes, and
dt_{2}
is expressed in terms of

dx_{5}
.
So the cotangent space is spanned by
dy_{2},dx_{5},dt_{5},dx_{6}
and is at most four-dimensional.

5.13
\mathcal{F}.1

We shall place the charts in different order, than what came up during the classification, to emphasize the symmetry of this configuration:

We choose the coordinates:

\small\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{1}}=\left({1:1+{x_{1}}:{y_{1}}}\right)\\
{B_{1}}=\left({1:1+{x_{1}}+{z_{1}}}\right.\\
\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\left.:{{y_{1}}+{z_{1}}{t_{1}}}\right)\\
{C_{1}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{D_{1}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{E_{1}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{F_{1}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{x_{1}},{y_{1}},{z_{1}}\sim 0\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{2}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{B_{2}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{C_{2}}=\left({1:1+{x_{2}}:{y_{2}}}\right)\\
{D_{2}}=\left({1:1+{x_{2}}+{z_{2}}}\right.\\
\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\left.:{{y_{2}}+{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}\right)\\
{E_{2}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{F_{2}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{x_{2}},{y_{2}},{z_{2}}\sim 0\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{3}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{3}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{C_{3}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{D_{3}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{E_{3}}=\left({1:1+{x_{3}}:{y_{3}}}\right)\\
{F_{3}}=\left({1:1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}}\right.\\
\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\left.:{{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\right)\\
{x_{3}},{y_{3}},{z_{3}}\sim 0\end{array}\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{4}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{4}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{C_{4}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{D_{4}}=\left({{x_{4}}{y_{4}}:1:{x_{4}}}\right)\\
{E_{4}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{F_{4}}=\left({{z_{4}}{t_{4}}:{z_{4}}:1}\right)\\
{x_{4}},{y_{4}},{z_{4}},{t_{4}}\sim 0\end{array}&\vline&*&\vline&*&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{7}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{7}}=\left({1:{x_{7}}:{x_{7}}}\right)\\
{C_{7}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{D_{7}}=\left({{y_{7}}:1:{y_{7}}}\right)\\
{E_{7}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{F_{7}}=\left({{z_{7}}{t_{7}}:{z_{7}}:1}\right)\\
{x_{7}},{y_{7}},{z_{7}}\sim 0\neq t_{7}\end{array}\end{array}

We shall show that the fourth chart is redundant;
because of the symmetry, so are the fifth and the sixth chart,
so we won’t introduce coordinates for those charts.

For the seventh chart all coordinates are zero, except for
t_{7}
.
If
t_{7}
becomes zero (or symmetrically,
\infty
) we get an even more degenerate
collection of charts which we shall discuss later.
So, for now, all coordinates except
t_{7}
are zeroes, while
t_{7}\neq 0
.

We have

{x_{4}}={\left[{C,E;D,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1+{x_{3}}\\
{y_{3}}\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{3}}}&1\\
{{y_{3}}}&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{3}}}&1\\
{{y_{3}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=-\frac{{{y_{3}}}}{{1+{x_{3}}-{y_{3}}}}

{y_{4}}={\left[{E,A;D,B}\right]_{C}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1+{x_{3}}\\
{y_{3}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 1}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{1+{x_{3}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
{1+{x_{3}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=-{x_{3}}

{z_{4}}={\left[{E,C;F,B}\right]_{A}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1+{x_{2}}\\
{y_{2}}\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 1}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{1+{x_{2}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
{1+{x_{2}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=-{x_{2}}

{t_{4}}={\left[{C,A;F,B}\right]_{E}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1+{x_{2}}\\
{y_{2}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{l}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{2}}}&1\\
{{y_{2}}}&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{2}}}&1\\
{{y_{2}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{-{y_{2}}}}{{1+{x_{2}}-{y_{2}}}}

So all coordinates related to the fourth chart are expressed explicitly from the other charts,
hence we may disregard the fourth chart, and for the similar reason the fifth and the sixth chart.
Now we shall relate charts 1-3 to each other. We may use the symmetry, so for each formula we write
there are two more symmetric formulas.

We have already noticed that
-{x_{3}}=\left[{E,A;D,B}\right]_{C}
. Hence

-{x_{3}}={\left[{E,A;D,B}\right]_{C}}=\small\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1&1\\
0&{1+{x_{2}}+{z_{2}}}&{1+{x_{2}}}\\
0&{{y_{2}}+{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}&{{y_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&1\\
0&1&{1+{x_{2}}}\\
1&1&{{y_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1&1\\
0&1&{1+{x_{2}}}\\
0&1&{{y_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&1\\
0&{1+{x_{2}}+{z_{2}}}&{1+{x_{2}}}\\
1&{{y_{2}}+{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}&{{y_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{z_{2}}}&{1+{x_{2}}}\\
{{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}&{{y_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
1&{1+{x_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{1+{x_{2}}}\\
1&{{y_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
{{z_{2}}}&{1+{x_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{1+{x_{2}}}\\
{{t_{2}}}&{{y_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot{x_{2}}}}{{\left({{y_{2}}-{x_{2}}-1}\right)\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&{1+{x_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{x_{2}}\left({{y_{2}}-{t_{2}}-{t_{2}}{x_{2}}}\right)}}{{1-{x_{2}}-{y_{2}}}}

Also

{y_{3}}={\left[{A,C;E,D}\right]_{B}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&1\\
0&0&1\\
1&0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1&1\\
{1+{x_{2}}}&{1+{x_{2}}+{z_{2}}}&1\\
{{y_{2}}}&{{y_{2}}+{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&1\\
0&{1+{x_{2}}+{z_{2}}}&1\\
1&{{y_{2}}+{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1&1\\
{1+{x_{2}}}&0&1\\
{{y_{2}}}&0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0&1\\
{1+{x_{2}}}&{{z_{2}}}&1\\
{{y_{2}}}&{{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
{{x_{2}}+{z_{2}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left({{y_{2}}-{x_{2}}-1}\right)}}=\frac{{{z_{2}}-{y_{2}}{z_{2}}+{x_{2}}{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}}{{\left({{x_{2}}+{z_{2}}}\right)\cdot\left({1+{x_{2}}-{y_{2}}}\right)}}

Now we shall express

{\left[{A,B;E,D}\right]_{F}}

in two different ways:

{\left[{A,B;E,D}\right]_{F}}=\small\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1&1\\
0&{1+{x_{3}}}&{1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}}\\
0&{{y_{3}}}&{{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&1\\
1&1&{1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}}\\
0&1&{{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1&1\\
0&1&{1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}}\\
0&1&{{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&1\\
1&{1+{x_{3}}}&{1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}}\\
0&{{y_{3}}}&{{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{3}}}&{{z_{3}}}\\
{{y_{3}}}&{{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left({1-{y_{3}}-{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\right)}}{{\left({{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}-\left({1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}}\right)}\right)\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&0\\
1&{1+{x_{3}}}&{{z_{3}}}\\
0&{{y_{3}}}&{{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{3}}}&1\\
{{y_{3}}}&{{t_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left({1-{y_{3}}-{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\right)}}{{\left({{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}-\left({1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}}\right)}\right)\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&0\\
1&{1+{x_{3}}}&1\\
0&{{y_{3}}}&{{t_{3}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=

=\frac{{\left({{t_{3}}+{t_{3}}{x_{3}}-{y_{3}}}\right)\left({1-{y_{3}}-{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\right)}}{{\left({1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}-{y_{3}}-{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\right)\cdot{t_{3}}}}

{\left[{A,B;E,D}\right]_{F}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
1&{{y_{2}}+{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&{{y_{2}}+{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=1-{y_{2}}-{z_{2}}{t_{2}}

Therefore

\frac{{\left({{t_{3}}+{t_{3}}{x_{3}}-{y_{3}}}\right)\left({1-{y_{3}}-{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\right)}}{{\left({1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}-{y_{3}}-{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\right)\cdot{t_{3}}}}=1-{y_{2}}-{z_{2}}{t_{2}}

We may multiply by denominators and skip the terms of second order, so we get

-{x_{3}}\approx-t_{2}x_{2}

0\approx{z_{2}}

{t_{3}}+{t_{3}}{x_{3}}-{y_{3}}-{t_{3}}{y_{3}}-{z_{3}}t_{3}^{2}\approx{t_{3}}\left({1-{y_{2}}-{z_{2}}{t_{2}}+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}-{y_{3}}-{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\right)

We may simplify these equations; in particular we may use that
z_{2}\approx 0
,
and by symmetry
z_{3}\approx 0\approx z_{1}
, which allows to simplify the third equation.

{x_{3}}\approx t_{2}x_{2}

0\approx{z_{2}}

{y_{3}}\approx{t_{3}}{y_{2}}

After simplifying and using symmetry, we get

\begin{array}[]{c}\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{3}}\approx{t_{2}}{x_{2}}\\
{y_{3}}\approx{t_{3}}{y_{2}}\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{1}}\approx{t_{3}}{x_{3}}\\
{y_{1}}\approx{t_{1}}{y_{3}}\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{x_{2}}\approx{t_{1}}{x_{1}}\\
{y_{2}}\approx{t_{2}}{y_{1}}\end{array}\end{array}\\
{z_{1}},{z_{2}},{z_{3}}\approx 0\end{array}

To get more equations we shall compute
\left[{A,C;F,D}\right]_{E}
in 3 different ways:

{\left[{A,C;F,D}\right]_{E}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1+{x_{1}}\\
{y_{1}}\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 1}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{1+{x_{1}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0\\
{1+{x_{1}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=-{x_{1}}

{\left[{A,C;F,D}\right]_{E}}={\left[\small{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1+{x_{2}}\\
{y_{2}}\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
1+{x_{2}}+{z_{2}}\\
{y_{2}}+{z_{2}}{t_{2}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 1\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 0}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{2}}}&{1+{x_{2}}+{z_{2}}}\\
{{y_{2}}}&{{y_{2}}+{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{1+{x_{2}}+{z_{2}}}\\
1&{{y_{2}}+{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{1+{x_{2}}}&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{-\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{1+{x_{2}}}&{{z_{2}}}\\
{{y_{2}}}&{{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left({1+{x_{2}}+{z_{2}}}\right)\cdot\left({1+{x_{2}}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{{y_{2}}{z_{2}}-{z_{2}}{t_{2}}\left({1+{x_{2}}}\right)}}{{\left({1+{x_{2}}+{z_{2}}}\right)\cdot\left({1+{x_{2}}}\right)}}

{\left[{A,C;F,D}\right]_{E}}={\left[{\left(\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
0\\
0\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
1\\
0\end{array}\right);\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{z_{7}}{t_{7}}\\
{z_{7}}\\
1\end{array}\right),\left(\begin{array}[]{c}{y_{7}}\\
1\\
{y_{7}}\end{array}\right)}\right]_{\left({\scriptstyle 0\atop{\scriptstyle 0\atop\scriptstyle 1}}\right)}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{z_{7}}{t_{7}}}\\
0&{{z_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{y_{7}}}\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{y_{7}}}\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{z_{7}}{t_{7}}}\\
1&{{z_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{{y_{7}}}}{{{t_{7}}}}

Therefore

-{x_{1}}={\left[{A,C;F,D}\right]_{E}}=\frac{{{y_{2}}{z_{2}}-{z_{2}}{t_{2}}\left({1+{x_{2}}}\right)}}{{\left({1+{x_{2}}+{z_{2}}}\right)\cdot\left({1+{x_{2}}}\right)}}\approx 0,

as we have seen that
z_{2}\approx 0
, and

-{x_{1}}={\left[{A,C;F,D}\right]_{E}}=\frac{{{y_{7}}}}{{{t_{7}}}}.

Hence
x_{1}\approx 0
and by symmetry
x_{2},x_{3}\approx 0
.
Also
y_{7}=-t_{7}x_{1}\approx 0
.

Now we write more formulas related to the seventh chart.
We have already seen that
{x_{3}}={\left[{E,A;D,B}\right]_{C}}
. Therefore

-{x_{3}}={\left[{E,A;D,B}\right]_{C}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{y_{7}}}\\
1&{{y_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&{{x_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&{{x_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{y_{7}}}\\
0&{{y_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot{x_{7}}}}{{-1\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}={x_{7}}

We also see that

{y_{3}}={\left[{A,C;E,D}\right]_{B}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0&1\\
0&0&{{x_{7}}}\\
0&1&{{x_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{y_{7}}}&1\\
1&1&{{x_{7}}}\\
0&{{y_{7}}}&{{x_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{y_{7}}}&1\\
0&1&{{x_{7}}}\\
0&{{y_{7}}}&{{x_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&0&1\\
1&0&{{x_{7}}}\\
0&1&{{x_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=

=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{x_{7}}}\\
1&{{x_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{y_{7}}}&1\\
1&1&{{x_{7}}}\\
0&{{y_{7}}}&{{x_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{x_{7}}}\\
{{y_{7}}}&{{x_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot 1}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{y_{7}}}&1\\
1&1&{{x_{7}}}\\
0&{{y_{7}}}&{{x_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
{{y_{7}}}&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot 1}}=

=\frac{{-\left({{y_{7}}-{x_{7}}{y_{7}}}\right)}}{{1-{y_{7}}}}

{y_{1}}={\left[{C,E;A,F}\right]_{D}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&{{y_{7}}}\\
1&0&1\\
0&0&{{y_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{z_{7}}{t_{7}}}&{{y_{7}}}\\
0&{{z_{7}}}&1\\
1&1&{{y_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{z_{7}}{t_{7}}}&{{y_{7}}}\\
1&{{z_{7}}}&1\\
0&1&{{y_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&{{y_{7}}}\\
0&0&1\\
1&0&{{y_{7}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=

=\frac{{-{y_{7}}\cdot{z_{7}}\left({{t_{7}}-{y_{7}}}\right)}}{{{y_{7}}\left({1-{z_{7}}{t_{7}}}\right)}}=\frac{-{{z_{7}}\left({{t_{7}}-{y_{7}}}\right)}}{{1-{z_{7}}{t_{7}}}}

By multiplying by denominators and dismissing the terms of second order we get

-x_{3}=x_{7}

y_{3}\approx-y_{7}

y_{1}\approx t_{7}z_{7}

Recall that

z_{1},z_{2},z_{3}\approx 0

x_{1},x_{2},x_{3}\approx 0

y_{7}\approx 0

Hence

x_{7}\approx-x_{3}\approx 0

y_{3}\approx-y_{7}\approx 0

{y_{1}}\approx{t_{1}}{y_{3}}\approx 0

{y_{2}}\approx{t_{2}}{y_{1}}\approx 0

And since
t_{7}\neq 0
, also

z_{7}=\frac{y_{1}}{t_{7}}\approx 0.

To summarize:
dx_{i},dy_{i},dz_{i}\approx 0
for
i\in\{1,2,3,7\}
.
Therefore the cotangent space is spanned by

dt_{1},dt_{2},dt_{3}
and
dt_{7}
.

5.14
\mathcal{F}.1^{\prime}

The last subcase we verify is a degeneration of the previous subcase,
when the triple ratio
\left\{{P_{1},P_{5},P_{3};P_{2},P_{6},P_{4}}\right\}
tends to zero;
if the triple ratio tends to infinity, it is symmetrical.

We choose the coordinates symmetrically, consistently with the previous subcase,
in order to use the formulas from the previous subcase.

\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{1}}=\left({1:1+{x_{1}}:{y_{1}}}\right)\\
{B_{1}}=\left({1:1+{x_{1}}+{z_{1}}}\right.\\
\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\left.{:{y_{1}}+{z_{1}}{t_{1}}}\right)\\
{C_{1}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{D_{1}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{E_{1}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{F_{1}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{x_{1}},{y_{1}},{z_{1}}\sim 0\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{2}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{B_{2}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{C_{2}}=\left({1:1+{x_{2}}:{y_{2}}}\right)\\
{D_{2}}=\left({1:1+{x_{2}}+{z_{2}}}\right.\\
\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\left.{:{y_{2}}+{z_{2}}{t_{2}}}\right)\\
{E_{2}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{F_{2}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{x_{2}},{y_{2}},{z_{2}}\sim 0\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{3}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{3}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{C_{3}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{D_{3}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{E_{3}}=\left({1:1+{x_{3}}:{y_{3}}}\right)\\
{F_{3}}=\left({1:1+{x_{3}}+{z_{3}}}\right.\\
\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\left.{:{y_{3}}+{z_{3}}{t_{3}}}\right)\\
{x_{3}},{y_{3}},{z_{3}}\sim 0\end{array}\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{4}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{4}}=\left({1:1:1}\right)\\
{C_{4}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{D_{4}}=\left({{x_{4}}{y_{4}}:1:{x_{4}}}\right)\\
{E_{4}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{F_{4}}=\left({{z_{4}}{t_{4}}:{z_{4}}:1}\right)\\
x_{4},y_{4},z_{4},t_{4}\sim 0\end{array}&\vline&*&\vline&*\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{7}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{7}}=\left({1:{x_{7}}:{x_{7}}}\right)\\
{C_{7}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{D_{7}}=\left({{y_{7}}:1:{y_{7}}}\right)\\
{E_{7}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{F_{7}}=\left({{z_{7}}{t_{7}}:{z_{7}}:1}\right)\\
x_{7},y_{7},z_{7},t_{7}\sim 0\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{8}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{8}}=\left({1:{z_{8}}{t_{8}}:{z_{8}}}\right)\\
{C_{8}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{D_{8}}=\left({{x_{8}}:1:{x_{8}}}\right)\\
{E_{8}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{F_{8}}=\left({{y_{8}}:{y_{8}}:1}\right)\\
x_{8},y_{8},z_{8},t_{8}\sim 0\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{A_{9}}=\left({1:0:0}\right)\\
{B_{9}}=\left({1:{y_{9}}:{y_{9}}}\right)\\
{C_{9}}=\left({0:1:0}\right)\\
{D_{9}}=\left({{z_{9}}:1:{z_{9}}{t_{9}}}\right)\\
{E_{9}}=\left({0:0:1}\right)\\
{F_{9}}=\left({{x_{9}}:{x_{9}}:1}\right)\\
x_{9},y_{9},z_{9},t_{9}\sim 0\end{array}\end{array}

As in previous case, the coordinates from the charts 4-6 are expressed from
the coordinates from the charts 1-3.

We can write again most of the formulas we had in the previous case for the same reasons, such as:

\begin{array}[]{c}\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{3}}\approx{t_{2}}{x_{2}}\\
{y_{3}}\approx{t_{3}}{y_{2}}\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{1}}\approx{t_{3}}{x_{3}}\\
{y_{1}}\approx{t_{1}}{y_{3}}\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{x_{2}}\approx{t_{1}}{x_{1}}\\
{y_{2}}\approx{t_{2}}{y_{1}}\end{array}\end{array}\\
{z_{1}},{z_{2}},{z_{3}}\approx 0\end{array}

As before we may also develop the formulas
x_{1}\approx 0
and
y_{7}=-t_{7}x_{1}\approx 0
, and now both
formulas have 3 symmetric versions. We still have
x_{7}=-x_{3}
and
y_{7}=-y_{3}
, so we may
conclude immediately that
x_{7}\approx 0
and
y_{3}\approx 0
, with two more symmetric versions
for both last formulas
have 3 symmetric versions

\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{1}}\approx 0\\
{y_{7}}\approx 0\\
x_{7}\approx 0\\
y_{1}\approx 0\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{c}{x_{2}}\approx 0\\
{y_{8}}\approx 0\\
x_{8}\approx 0\\
y_{2}\approx 0\end{array}&\vline&\begin{array}[]{l}{x_{3}}\approx 0\\
{y_{9}}\approx 0\\
x_{9}\approx 0\\
y_{3}\approx 0\end{array}\end{array}

The treatment of
z_{7}
in the previous case relied on division by
t_{7}
which is zero in this case,
so it can’t be simply repeated. However

{z_{7}}={\left[{E,C;F,B}\right]_{A}}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{x_{9}}}\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{y_{9}}}\\
0&{{y_{9}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{y_{9}}}\\
1&{{y_{9}}}\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{x_{9}}}\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=\frac{{-{x_{9}}\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&1\\
0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1\\
1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot 1}}=-{x_{9}},

and there are two more symmetric versions of this, hence also
z_{i}\approx 0
for
i\in\left\{7,8,9\right\}
.
Therefore
dx_{i}=dy_{i}=dz_{i}=0
for
i\in\left\{1,2,3,7,8,9\right\}
.
So, the cotangent space is spanned by
dt_{1},dt_{2},dt_{3},dt_{7},dt_{8},dt_{9}
.

We may express the triple ratio
\left\{P_{1},P_{5},P_{3};P_{2},P_{6},P_{4}\right\}
by
coordinates of the seventh chart:

\left\{{A,E,C;B,F,D}\right\}=\frac{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&{{z_{7}}{t_{7}}}&0\\
1&{{z_{7}}}&0\\
0&1&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}0&1&1\\
0&{{x_{7}}}&0\\
1&{{x_{7}}}&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&{{y_{7}}}&0\\
0&1&1\\
0&{{y_{7}}}&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}{{\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{z_{7}}{t_{7}}}&1&0\\
{{z_{7}}}&0&0\\
1&0&1\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}1&0&1\\
{{x_{7}}}&1&0\\
{{x_{7}}}&0&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|\cdot\left|{\left({\begin{array}[]{*{20}{c}}{{y_{7}}}&0&0\\
1&0&1\\
{{y_{7}}}&1&0\end{array}}\right)}\right|}}=

=\frac{{-{z_{7}}{t_{7}}\cdot{x_{7}}\cdot{y_{7}}}}{{{-z_{7}}\cdot{x_{7}}\cdot{y_{7}}}}={t_{7}}

We could do it similarly using the eighth or the ninth chart, and conclude that
t_{7}=t_{8}=t_{9}
.
Hence the cotangent space is spanned by
dt_{1},dt_{2},dt_{3}
and
dt_{7}
, hence it is at most four-dimensional.

To summarize: our moduli space is smooth at all points.
